# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
Apply limit x to infinity to find horizontal asymptote ^uK1j1gep

All the terms with x in the denominator tend to 0 ^6Gyl9RgU

Thus the horizontal asymptote is at y = 0 ^0dGBr5y3

All the terms with x in the denominator tend to 0 ^VySN4umD

Thus the horizontal asymptotes are at 1 and -1 ^UvxrMx7S

All the terms with x in the denominator tend to 0 ^QZPiWumv

To check if this function has a the horizontal asymptote or not, we need to see if both parts of this function have a horizontal asymptote in the same direction.
Let's start with negative infinity ^NVbUKls9

Both 2^{-x} and values with x in denominator tend to 0, so   ^MnLYbioj

Thus the horizontal asymptote is at y = 2 ^0LWukyBG

Horizontal Asymptotes ^8FHBKmqa

Examples ^5K76KjTi
